010952:         "dry_run": "true" if dry_run else "false",
010953:         # Existing app ports are already in use by this same app. Do not reject
010954:         # an update just because the current app owns them.

010956:         # ZimaOS merges query parameters into MessageBus event properties.
010957:         # Supplying app:name lets app:install-progress map back to this card.
010958:         "app:name": str(compose_project),

010961:         f"/v2/app_management/compose/{app_id}?{query}",

020005: function renderUninstallPanel(app,index){
020006:   // Folder-preservation semantics are provided by the official ZimaOS
020007:   // delete_config_folder parameter. Local/non-ZimaOS containers do not expose
020008:   // that app-folder concept, so the control is shown only for ZimaOS apps.
020009:   if(!app.compose_project)return '';
020010: 
020011:   const busy=state.uninstallingApps.has(app.stack_key);
020012:   const selfProtected=appSelfProtected(app);
020013:   const blocked=busy||selfProtected;
020014:   const progressView=actionProgressView(app.stack_key,'uninstall');
020015:   const progressClass=busy&&progressView.hasPercent?' has-percent':'';
020016:   const progressStyle=busy&&progressView.hasPercent?` style="--action-progress:${progressView.progress}%"`:'';
020017:   const buttonLabel=busy?actionProgressLabel('uninstall',progressView.progress,progressView.phase):safeUninstallButtonLabel();
020018:   const helpText=selfProtected?t('selfProtectionUninstall'):t('uninstallFolderHelp');
020019:   const protectionTitle=selfProtected?` title="${esc(selfProtectionTitle())}"`:'';
020020:

020022:     <div class="uninstall-panel-title">${esc(t('uninstallTitle'))}</div>
020023:     <label class="uninstall-folder-option">
020024:       <input type="checkbox" data-uninstall-folder-index="${index}" ${blocked?'disabled':''}>
020025:       <span>${esc(t('uninstallDeleteFolder'))}</span>
020026:     </label>
020027:     <div class="uninstall-folder-help">${esc(helpText)}</div>
020028:     <button class="uninstall-button ${busy?'action-progress':''}${progressClass}"${progressStyle}${protectionTitle} type="button" data-uninstall-index="${index}" data-progress-stack="${esc(app.stack_key)}" data-progress-kind="uninstall" ${blocked?'disabled':''}>${esc(buttonLabel)}</button>
020029:   </section>`;
